- **understand-stream_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_log` **DEFINITION:** An immutable append-only sequence of events retained on disk or equivalent durable storage; unlike pubsub_message which is ephemeral, events in a stream_log persist for the configured retention period and can be reread from any offset by any consumer group. - **understand-stream_offset**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_offset` **DEFINITION:** A monotonically increasing integer position marker assigned to each event within a partition, uniquely identifying the event's place in the partition's sequence and enabling consumers to resume reading from a specific point. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_offset) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_partition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_partition` **DEFINITION:** A distinct, ordered slice of an event stream, identified by a partition key or index, enabling parallel production and consumption; each partition maintains its own offset sequence independently of other partitions. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_partition) · ★1)

([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_design_patterns/understand-structural_pattern) · ★1)
- **understand-subtree_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.subtree_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 040000; entry points to a nested tree object representing a directory; the tree entry name contains a forward slash but the slash is not stored in the entry itself. - **understand-symlink_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.symlink_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 120000; entry points to a blob object containing the target path as raw bytes; the blob content is the symlink destination, not the link data itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-symlink_mode) · ★1)
- **understand-synchronizes_with**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.synchronizes_with : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), deep_c11_memory_model(1)` **DEFINITION:** A relation between atomic operations in memory models where a release fence on one thread synchronizes with an acquire fence on another thread, establishing happens-before order across threads. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with) · ★1)
- **understand-synchronizes_with_absence**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.synchronizes_with_absence : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A relaxed atomic operation does not synchronize with any other operation; it establishes no happens-before or synchronizes-with relationship with any thread, including the thread that performed the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with_absence) · ★1)
- **understand-tagged_union_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `type_systems_for_working_programmers.tagged_union_type : deep_tagged_union_type(23)` **DEFINITION:** Discriminated union with explicit tag field enabling exhaustive pattern matching. - **understand-tbox**: **CALL NUMBER:** `knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies.tbox` **DEFINITION:** The terminological box — the schema-level component of a knowledge base defining classes, properties, and their relations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies/understand-tbox) · ★1)
- **understand-te_capturing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_capturing` **DEFINITION:** The first phase of the CODE framework where information is quickly captured before it fades from working memory; a frictionless gateway that accepts raw input without requiring immediate structuring. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_capturing) · ★1)
- **understand-te_distilling**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_distilling` **DEFINITION:** The process of extracting essential insights from source material through progressive summarization; a compression operation that retains meaning while reducing volume. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_distilling) · ★1)
- **understand-te_expressing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_expressing` **DEFINITION:** The final phase of the CODE framework where accumulated knowledge becomes tangible output; the conversion operation that transforms internal knowledge structures into external artifacts. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_expressing) · ★1)
- **understand-te_organizing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_organizing` **DEFINITION:** The structuring operation that transforms captured material into a retrievable system; grouping related content by semantic relationship to enable future discovery and connection. - **understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_contention_surge` **DEFINITION:** A transient spike in the rate of lock or resource acquisition attempts triggered by mass wakeup, producing queue buildup and wasted retry cycles. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_retry_coordination` **DEFINITION:** The phenomenon where concurrently woken threads retry a failing operation in near-lockstep, reinforcing the contention surge; mitigated by jitter applied to retry delays. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_wait_set**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wait_set` **DEFINITION:** A collection of threads blocked on a single synchronization point awaiting a condition; the structural prerequisite for thundering-herd because coordinated wakeup of this set is the failure mode. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wait_set) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wakeup_event` **DEFINITION:** A single signal or broadcast that causes ALL members of a wait_set to transition from blocked to runnable simultaneously; the atomic cause of the thundering-herd pattern. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_checkpoint_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_checkpoint_record` **DEFINITION:** A special log_record written periodically by a checkpoint_writer; records the volatile database state at a consistent point, bounding the recovery_manager's replay horizon. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_checkpoint_record) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_flush_to_disk**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_flush_to_disk` **DEFINITION:** The act of transferring the contents of tl_log_buffer to durable storage; triggered by transaction commit, tl_log_buffer overflow, or a periodic checkpoint; O(1) or O(n) bounded by buffer size. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_flush_to_disk) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_log_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_log_record` **DEFINITION:** The fundamental unit of a transaction_log: a single append-only entry recording one atomic change or lifecycle event made by a transaction; identified by an lsn. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_log_record) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_recovery_manager**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_recovery_manager` **DEFINITION:** The subsystem that, on system restart, reads the transaction_log from the last tl_checkpoint_record forward; replays committed tl_redo_log entries and rolls back uncommitted transactions using tl_undo_log. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_recovery_manager) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_undo_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_undo_log` **DEFINITION:** A structured collection of tl_update_record entries chained by a transaction; traversed in reverse order during rollback to restore the before_image of each modified entity. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_undo_log) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_update_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_update_record` **DEFINITION:** A log_record capturing a before_image and after_image of a modified data page or row; used by both redo_recovery (for durability) and undo_recovery (for atomicity rollback). ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_update_record) · ★1)
- **understand-token_boundary_marker**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_context_window.token_boundary_marker` **DEFINITION:** The positional delimiter marking the transition from input context to output generation; tokens before this marker are provided context, tokens after are to be generated. - **understand-token_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.token_type` **DEFINITION:** OAuth 2.0 parameter indicating how the access_token is to be presented to the resource server, typically Bearer, specifying the authorization scheme to use in requests. - **understand-until-simple**: Use this skill BEFORE writing code for any task you're tempted to call "hard / tough / complex", any "why is X slow / where's the bottleneck", any "should I optimize Y", any concurrency/perf/kernel/scheduler investigation, or any time you catch yourself hand-waving a root cause. It is the pre-implementation understanding GATE — decompose to the atomic (line/kernel/buffer) level, get MEASURED evidence, let measurement correct your hypotheses, until the problem is SIMPLE and quantified. Only then write code. If it still feels hard, you haven't decomposed enough. ([acupof-ai/arle](https://github.com/acupof-ai/arle/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/understand-until-simple) · ★19)
